Pseudo:
def toggle_delta(h, p, b, n):
    L=2*n-1
    d = np.zeros(L, dtype=int32) # maybe int16
    center = n-1
    # first-factor p as a:
    # lags l from p to center+p inclusive; q = p - (l-center) = center+p - l.
    # For b=0: value = 1 - h_new[q], with new h_q = h_old q except q=p? since only p changes b0->1; for q=p value 0.
    # If q != p: 1 - h[q]. For b=0, h[p]=0, so base 1-h[q] gives q=p 1, need set 0.
    # If b=1: value = -(1 - h_old[q]); q=p: 0; base with h[p]=1 gives 0, okay no set? 1-h[p]=0.
    # l range: first_l = p, last = center + p. Let q = center+p - l. If l=p -> q=center; l=center+p -> q=0.
    # Its reversed slice of h? We can create q array? To avoid Python loop, use h.indexing with reverse slice.
    # For l in [p, center+p], q = center+p - l. q runs center down to 0. That's only valid if p? q max center? But q should 0..n-1. Why center? n-1 center, yes q ranges 0..n-1? For l=p, q=center; not n-1? If p not? Let's check n=5 center4, p=1: lags first? p-as-first q=p-s valid s=-p..n-1-p => l from center+s = center-p to center+n-1-p? Hmm earlier maybe wrong.

Swap new_corr function:
def swap_new_corr(corr, h, i, j, n, center, L):
    new = corr.copy()
    apply_toggle_inplace(new, h, i, 0, n, center, L)
    apply_toggle_inplace(new, h, j, 1, n, center, L)
    new[center + j - i] += 1
    new[center + i - j] += 1
    return new
But careful: apply_toggle for j base uses h old where i=0. It doesn't know i toggled; corrections added. Good.

Function binary_local_search(h, n, deadline, seed, mode):
Could include:
- initialize h int32 with exactly n/2 ones.
- corr, M.
- maintain zeros/ones indices (np.where).
- For a number of iterations until time:
   choose move type:
    a) random swap: i random zero, j random one.
    b) targeted: select a lag s at maximum (one of max lags). Try to reduce it by choosing i,j that affects that lag.
Need accept based on new M. Use simulated annealing: accept if deltaM <=0; else with prob exp(-deltaM/T) maybe T in count units (e.g. 2 to 0). Since M integer, worse by 1/2. Use random restarts/patience.

But random swaps may plateau low. We can do exact best improvement among a subset:
- For each of top q lags (M or M-1), evaluate possible swaps that reduce that lag significantly. We can compute delta for that lag for all i,j? For a fixed lag s, new corr_s = old + effect_i_j at s. The effect on a single lag from swap can be computed O(1) maybe. Then choose swap that minimizes max? Let's derive effect on specific s for candidate i,j without full delta. This can speed targeted search.

For shift s, terms affected if i or j is first/second in the overlapping pairs. Specifically old_corr_s = sum over pairs. New_corr_s = old - old_terms + new_terms for terms involving i/j. There are at most 4 base terms plus cross. We can compute directly using h before/after for that s:
Given s, valid pairs: if s>=0: p from s to n-1, q=p-s. If s<0: p from 0 to n-1+ s, q=p-s (|s|). A pair index corresponds to one p.
A swap i 0->1, j1->0 affects corr_s by sum over affected pairs if their p or q equals i or j. For each affected pair (p,q), term after - before. Could compute by enumerating up to 4 pairs:
- pair with p=i (if valid): q=i-s.
- pair with q=i (if valid): p=i+s.
- pair with p=j.
- pair with q=j.
But avoid double counting same pair if p/q both i/j (cross i,j). Simpler: old terms for pairs in affected set, new terms.
We can precompute for each s list of pair positions? For a targeted s, we can compute Delta_s(i,j) quickly by cases.

Let's implement binary_search in detail.

Input h0 int array? We'll convert to int binary with exactly n/2 ones. n even.
Initialize:
target = n//2
h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
if h0 sum not target: idx = np.argsort(h0)[::-1]; h[idx[:target]]=1.
corr = np.correlate(h.astype(np.int32), 1-h.astype(np.int32), mode='full').astype(np.int32)
M = int(corr.max())
C = M * 2.0 / n
best_M=M, best_h=h.copy()
zeros = np.where(h==0)[0], ones = np.where(h==1)[0]
rng.

Temperature: T = 2.0 maybe, cool to 0.2. Use iterations until deadline. Each iteration:
- choose strategy based on time/iteration: 20% targeted, 80% random? But targeted can only use O(n) eval.
Random:
  i = zeros[rand], j = ones[rand]
  new = swap_new_corr(...)
  newM = int(new.max())
  dp = newM - M
  if dp <0 or (T>0 and rng.random()< exp(-dp/T)): accept.
Accept:
  h[i]=1; h[j]=0; corr = new; M = newM; update zeros/ones by removing i? Need maintain arrays.
Simpler recompute zeros/ones after every accept with np.where (n length) cheaper. If rejects no update.
If improvement: best update, no_improve=0 else no_improve++.
But if accepted worse, no_improve? For SA, maybe no_improve increments only if newM>=best_M. Kicks on no_improve patience.

Targeted:
  Use current corr. Get max_lags = np.where(corr==M)[0]. Choose up to 5 random lags among them (or all if few). For each lag:
    s = lag - center. Determine affected pairs (p,q) and find indices where h[p]=1, h[q]=0 (to reduce). We need candidate i (zero) = q? j (one)=p? For positive s: p=q+s, h_p=1,h_q=0. Swapping j=p (1 to 0) and i=q (0 to1) changes that pair from 1 to0? Let's verify: term p first (right), q second (left). If h_p=1,h_q=0, old term1. After swap p=0,q=1 term0, yes reduce this lag by at least1. Also other terms. So candidate (i=q, j=p). For negative s similar but p<q? Pair p first (left), q second? For s negative, s = p-q; q=p-s = p+|s|. h_p=1,h_q=0. Swapping j=p, i=q also? First p becomes0, second q becomes1, old term1, new0. Yes.
So for any lag, select pairs with h_p=1, h_q=0; candidate i=q, j=p. Good.
How to get p,q for lag efficiently:
if s>=0: q range 0..n-1-s; p=q+s.
 ones_p = np.where(h[q+s:]==1)[0] + (q+s start)
 zeros_q = np.where(h[:n-s]==0)[0]
 Need pairs both: ones_p positions correspond to q; zeros_q positions. Candidate q in intersection of q where h[q+s]=1 and h[q]=0. We can compute mask = (h[0:n-1-s]==0) & (h[s:n]==1). q_idx = np.where(mask)[0]; p_idx=q_idx+s. Then random choose up to e.g. 10 q. This vector length n-s. Fine for n=500.
For s<0: let d=-s, p range 0..n-1-d, q=p+d. mask = (h[0:n-1-d]==1) & (h[d:n]==0); p_idx=where, q_idx=p_idx+d.
Then candidates i=q_idx, j=p_idx.
Evaluate full swap for maybe random 8 candidates per chosen lag. Keep bestM, if any <M accept; else if within T maybe accept best? For descending, only accept if improves current M (strict) to move fast; or accept if newM < M or SA with T. To avoid plateau, accept if newM < M + small.

Kicks:
When no_improve > patience (e.g., 20000 random+targeted iterations), do:
  k = rng.randint(3,8)

  recompute corr
  T = min(T_start, T*3)
  no_improve=0

def smooth_c5(h, alpha):
  g = np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full')*(2/n)
  return float(g.max()? plus soft)

Alternative simple basin-hopping on fractional: coordinate descent with single coordinate minimization. For a given coordinate r, objective max_s (A_s + B_s*h_r + D_s*h_r^2) where D_s = -1 if pair with itself? For each shift where r appears as first or second, the expression is linear in h_r except diagonal term h(1-h) at center where r appears both first and second? Actually diagonal C_0 includes h_r(1-h_r) = h_r -h_r^2; other shifts linear. The max of a finite set of affine or concave quadratics (at center). We can find exact minimizer over [0,1] for fixed others and sum? Sum constraint complicates; if we adjust coordinate, sum changes. Use scipy minimize L-BFGS-B with penalty for sum:
def cont_objective(x):
  h=x
  g=np.correlate(h,1-h)*dx
  m=g.max(); sh=g-m; soft=(sm + logsumexp)/alpha?
  penalty = mu*(h.sum()-target)^2
  return m + soft/alpha + penalty
Gradient maybe not provided; use finite? L-BFGS-B without jac slow. Could use SLSQP with no jac finite difference n=250, too slow.
Implement analytical gradient for augmented objective:
obj = logsumexp(beta*g)/(beta) maybe. Let z = beta*g, zmax, q=exp(z-zmax), Q=sum q. S= (zmax+logQ)/beta.
∂S/∂h_r = (1/beta)∑ q_s ∂g_s? Actually derivative of logsumexp: (1/Q)∑ q ∂(β g_s)/(∂h)= (1/Q)∑ q β ∂g_s = (β/Q)∑ q ∂g_s. Then /β = ∑ (q/Q) ∂g_s. So grad_S = ∑ w_s ∂g_s, where w_s normalized softmax(g_s).
Penalty P=mu(target - sum h)^2, grad penalty = -2mu(target - sum h) (all elements).
Bounds [0,1]. We can use scipy.optimize.minimize L-BFGS-B with this jac, penalty mu.
Need project? L-BFGS-B respects bounds; penalty drives sum. Good.
Compute grad_g efficiently.
Let g_s (not dx?) Can include dx constant. Let g_s = dx * c_s where c_s = correlate count. ∂g_s/∂h_r = dx * d_c_s.
We need w_s length L, sum1.
For each r, val = dx * (first_contrib - second_contrib):
first: sum over s where r is first with weight w_s*(1-h_q)
second: sum over s where r is second with weight w_s*h_p
We can compute by accumulating using shifts. Since n≤500, a double loop over s and vector slice maybe:
first_accum = np.zeros(n); second_accum=np.ones? 
For s_idx=0..L-1:
  s=s_idx-center

Potential problem with smooth_cpp_jac function using `np.correlate` and loops. Need implement efficiently. Let's write:
def smooth_cpp_obj(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')*dx
    z=beta*c
    zm=z.max()
    q=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=(zm+np.log(q.sum()))/beta
    return s+mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
def smooth_cpp_jac(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, mode='full')*dx
    z=beta*c
    zm=z.max()
    q=np.exp(z-zm)
    w=q/q.sum()
    L=2*n-1
    center=n-1
    fac=w*dx
    first=np.zeros(n)
    second=np.zeros(n)
    # loop s from -(n-1) to n-1
    # for lag index lidx: s = lidx-center
    for idx in range(L):
        s=idx-center
        acc=fac[idx]
        if acc == 0.0: continue (w small maybe, but computing exact; can skip if <1e-15)
        if s >=0:
            if s < n:
                first[s:n] += acc * (1.0 - x[0:n-s])
                second[0:n-s] += acc * x[s:n]
        else:
            d=-s
            if d < n:
                first[0:n-d] += acc * (1.0 - x[d:n])
                second[d:n] += acc * x[0:n-d]
    penalty=2.0*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)
    return first - second + penalty
Check Numpy slice shapes: first[s:n] length n-s; (1-x[0:n-s]) length match. second[0:n-s] length n-s. Good.
For s negative d, first[0:n-d] with q=x[d:n]; second[d:n] with p=x[0:n-d]. Good.
Note for s=0: first[0:n] += acc*(1-x); second[0:n] += acc*x; grad diag = first-second = acc*(1-2x), correct derivative of h(1-h) is 1-2h. Good.
This loop has Python if; 999 slips. We can optimize by precomputing ranges? Not necessary.
But inside loop, `acc=fac[idx]` is numpy scalar, multiplication creates temp. 1000*500 =500k temp, okay. Could skip very small weights for speed: if acc < 1e-16: continue. With beta moderate w maybe uniform 0.002, acc~0.0008; all included. Fine.

def project_feasible(h, target=None, n=None):
  h=np.asarray(h,float); n=len; target=0.5*n if None
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
       room=1-h; s=room.sum(); if s<1e-15: break; h += diff/s*room
    else: s=h.sum(); if s<1e-15: break; h += diff/s*h
  return np.clip(h,0,1)
This can slightly violate bounds due float but clip.

 return float(np.max(c)*dx)

def binary_search(h_start, n, deadline, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    target=n//2
    if len(h_start)!=n: h_start=interpolate(h_start,n)
    h=round_to_binary(h_start,n)
    corr=corr_int(h)
    M=int(corr.max())
    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n
    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    L=2*n-1; center=n-1
    T_start=3.0; T_end=0.15; patience=30000
    total_iters estimated? We can cool based on iterations: `rate=(T_end/T_start)**(1.0/200000.0)`; T=T_start
    no_improve=0
    # first do a quick greedy pass? maybe no.
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # choose targeted if no_improve < patience*0.5? else random
       if use_target and (no_improve > 2000 or rng.random()<0.15):
          # targeted
          if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
          maxidx=np.where(corr==M)[0]
          # choose up to 2 random lags
          if len(maxidx) <= 2: lags=maxidx
          else: lags=rng.choice(maxidx, size=min(2,len(maxidx)), replace=False)
          cand_best=None; cand_best_M=M; cand_i=-1; cand_j=-1
          samples=4
          for lag in lags:
              s=lag-center
              candidx = get_candidate_pairs_for_lag(h,s,center,n) returns arrays i_list, j_list? To avoid dh.
              cnt=len(i_list)
              if cnt==0: continue
              take=min(samples,cnt)
              pos=rng.choice(cnt, size=take, replace=False)
              for pos in pos:
                  ii=int(i_list[pos]); jj=int(j_list[pos])
                  new=swap_new_corr(corr,h,ii,jj,n,center,L)
                  nm=int(new.max())
                  if nm < cand_best_M:
                     cand_best_M=nm; cand_best=new; cand_i=ii; cand_j=jj
          if cand_best is not None:
             # accept if cand_best_M < M or SA
             delta=cand_best_M-M
             if delta<0 or (T>1e-9 and rng.random()<np.exp(-delta/T)):
                h[cand_i]=1; h[cand_j]=0; corr=cand_best; M=cand_best_M
                zeros, ones update recompute
                if M<best_M: best...
                else no_improve +=1
             else: no_improve +=1
          else: no_improve+=1
       else:
          # random
          i=zero; j=one
          new=swap_new_corr(...)
          nm=new.max(); delta=nm-M
          if delta<0 or exp accept:
             update
       T *= rate; if T<T_end: T=T_end
       if no_improve > patience:
          # kick
          nk=5+rng.randint(8)
          for _ in range(nk): random swap in-place
          # optional block swap
          if rng.random()<0.3: block_swap
          corr=corr_int(h); M=int(corr.max()); zeros/ones
          if M<best_M: update
          no_improve=0
          T=min(T_start, T*4)
       # periodic re-evaluate best? no
    return best_h, best_M, best_c

`run_length_start` must return int32. Implementation:
def run_length_start(n, seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    vals=[]; total=0; bit=rng.randint(2)
    while total < n:
        # geometric lengths 1.. maybe 5
        L = 1 + rng.randint(0,4)  # 1-5 uniform; or rng.geometric(0.35) cap5
        if total+L>n: L=n-total
        for _ in range(L): vals.append(bit); total+=L
        bit = 1-bit
    h=np.array(vals[:n], dtype=np.int32)
    target=n//2
    cnt=int(h.sum())
    if cnt>target: flip random ones to zero cnt-target
 elif cnt<target: flip zeros to one
 return h
Could use loop for L; n small. To reduce run lengths maybe use `L = int(rng.geometric(0.4))+1` cap 6.

`project_feasible`: Use target arg maybe.
```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    target=0.5*n
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    for _ in range(100):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; s=room.sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/s)*room
       else:
          s=h.sum()
          if s<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/s)*h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If n int.

`round_to_binary`:
```
def round_to_binary(h,n):
    target=n//2
    idx=np.argsort(h.astype(float), kind='mergesort')[::-1]
    out=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32)
    out[idx[:target]]=1
    return out
```
Use kind? okay.

`interpolate_vec`:
```
def interpolate_vec(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float)
    if len(v)==n: return v.copy()
    if len(v)==1: return np.full(n,v[0])
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(v))
    y=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(y,x,v)
```

`run_length_start`:
```
def run_length_start(n,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32)
    bit=rng.randint(2)
    i=0
    while i<n:
       L=int(rng.geometric(0.45))+1
       if L>6: L=6
       if i+L>n: L=n-i
       h[i:i+L]=bit
       i+=L
       bit=1-bit
    target=n//2
    cnt=int(h.sum())
    if cnt>target:
       idx=np.where(h==1)[0]
       flip=rng.choice(idx,cnt-target,replace=False)
       h[flip]=0
    elif cnt<target:
       idx=np.where(h==0)[0]
       flip=rng.choice(idx,target-cnt,replace=False)
       h[flip]=1
    return h
```
If rng.choice requires idx len enough; yes half ones/zeros maybe if cnt? If geometric yields cnt > n-target? With cap and bit alternation, cnt within [n/2-run?], likely enough. If not, fallback flip any. Add safety if len idx insufficient use all.

`corr_int`:
```
def corr_int(h):
    a=h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    b=(1-a).astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    return np.correlate(a,b,mode='full').astype(np.int32)
```
If h int32, 1-a int32.

`apply_toggle_inplace` as above. Use h int32. `hrev = h[::-1]` view but negative step; `arr[p:p+n] += hrev` works? For negative stride view, ufunc might be okay but for non-contiguous slices it can be slower. Could precompute `hrev = h[::-1]` each call. Fine. But `arr[p:p+n] += h[::-1]` where h[::-1] length n. For b=0 arr[p:p+n] -= h[::-1]. Good. To avoid creating array for `1` operations: adding scalar 1 okay.
```
def apply_toggle_inplace(arr,h,p,b,n,center,L):
    rev=h[::-1]
    if b==0:
        arr[p:p+n] += 1
        arr[p:p+n] -= rev
        arr[center-p:L-p] -= h
    else:
        arr[p:p+n] += rev
        arr[p:p+n] -= 1
        arr[center-p:L-p] += h
    arr[center] -= 1
```
Check b=0 second slice should subtract h forward, yes. But for b=0, p old 0, diagonal first base +1, subtract center once. What if the first and second slice overlap more than center; could the center subtract be double? No, only one diag. Good.
But for b=1, first slice adds rev -1; center first term = h[p]-1=0; second adds h; center term=h[p]=1; subtract. Good.

`swap_new_corr`:
```
def swap_new_corr(corr,h,i,j,n,center,L):
    new=corr.copy()
    apply_toggle_inplace(new,h,i,0,n,center,L)
    apply_toggle_inplace(new,h,j,1,n,center,L)
    new[center+j-i] += 1
    new[center+i-j] += 1
    return new
```
Need if i,j random maybe same? zeros/ones disjoint, no. Cross lag index within 0..L-1.

`lag_pairs`:
```
def lag_pairs(h,s,n):
    if s>=0:
       if s==0: return np.array([]),np.array([])
       mask=(h[:n-s]==0)&(h[s:]==1)
       q=np.where(mask)[0]
       return q, q+s
    else:
       d=-s
       if d==0: return empty
       mask=(h[:n-d]==1)&(h[d:]==0)
       p=np.where(mask)[0]
       return p+d, p
```
Return int arrays. For s positive, p=q+s. Good. For negative, i=q=p+d, j=p. That matches j-i=s? j(p) - i(p+d) = -d = s, yes.

Binary_search implementation. Need be careful with dtype `M` int. `corr` int32. `new.max()` returns np.int32; cast int.

```
def binary_search(h_start,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if len(h_start)!=n: h_start=interpolate_vec(h_start,n)
    h=round_to_binary(h_start,n)
    center=n-1; L=2*n-1
    corr=corr_int(h); M=int(corr.max())
    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n
    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    T=3.0; Tend=0.15; rate=(Tend/T)**(1.0/250000.0)
    patience=40000; no_imp=0
    targetC=0.3808; max_allowed=int(targetC*n/2 + 1e-9)
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
       # occasionally check if M already lower bound? no
       use_target = (no_imp > 3000 and no_imp % 10 == 0) or (rng.random() < 0.12)
       moved=False
       if use_target:
           maxidx=np.where(corr==M)[0]
           k=min(2,len(maxidx))
           lags=rng.choice(maxidx,k,replace=False) if len(maxidx)>1 else maxidx
           bi=-1; bj=-1; bm=M; bcorr=None
           for lag in lags:
               i_list,j_list=lag_pairs(h,lag-center,n)
               cnt=len(i_list)
               if cnt==0: continue
               t=min(6,cnt)
               pos=rng.choice(cnt,t,replace=False) if cnt>1 else np.array([0])
               for pos in pos:
                   ii=int(i_list[pos]); jj=int(j_list[pos])
                   ncorr=swap_new_corr(corr,h,ii,jj,n,center,L)
                   nm=int(ncorr.max())
                   if nm<bm:
                       bm=nm; bi=ii; bj=jj; bcorr=ncorr
           if bi>=0:
               delta=bm-M
               if delta<0 or (T>1e-9 and rng.random()<np.exp(-delta/T)):
                   h[bi]=1; h[bj]=0; corr=bcorr; M=bm; moved=True
                   if M<best_M: ...; no_imp=0 if improved best else no_imp+=1
                   else no_imp+=1
                   zeros=...; ones=...
               else: no_imp+=1
           else:
               no_imp+=1
       else:
           i=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); j=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
           ncorr=swap_new_corr(corr,h,i,j,n,center,L); nm=int(ncorr.max()); delta=nm-M
           if delta<0 or (T>1e-9 and rng.random()<np.exp(-delta/T)):
               h[i]=1; h[j]=0; corr=ncorr; M=nm
               if M<best_M: ... else no_imp+=1
               zeros=...; ones=...
           else: no_imp+=1
       T=max(Tend,T*rate)
       if no_imp > patience:
           # kick
           nk=5+rng.randint(10)
           for _ in range(nk):
              ii=...; jj=...; h[ii]=1; h[jj]=0
           if rng.random()<0.4:
              bl=3+rng.randint(max(1,n//10))
              if bl < n//2:
                 while True:
                    a=rng.randint(n-bl); b=rng.randint(n-bl)
                    if abs(a-b)>=bl: break
                    if ...
                    if rng.random()<0? fallback
                 tmp=h[a:a+bl].copy(); h[a:a+bl]=h[b:b+bl]; h[b:b+bl]=tmp
           corr=corr_int(h); M=int(corr.max())
           if M<best_M: update
           zeros=...; ones=...; no_imp=0; T=min(3.0,T*4)
       # if M reached absolute min? maybe no.
    return best_h.astype(float), best_M, best_c
```

Now continuous functions. Need avoid recomputing `q=q/gau.sum()` if sum zero? exp of zeros sum >0.
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,beta,mu):
   dx=2.0/n
   c=np.correlate(x,1.0-x,mode='full')*dx
   z=beta*c
   zmax=float(z.max())
   q=np.exp(z-zmax)
   val=(zmax+math.log(q.sum()))/beta
   return val+mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
```
`math.log` okay. If q.sum() 0 impossible.
`smooth_jac` as above. Use local variables for speed.
In jac loop, `if acc < 1e-18: continue` to skip. But if beta moderate, many acc >. Use 1e-18.
Add penalty: `pen=2.0*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)` return first-second+pen.
Need h=x float. If x not contiguous? okay.
Potential `np.correlate` with float uses O(n^2). Could be heavy but okay.

`continuous_refine`:
```
def continuous_refine(h0,n,deadline,seed):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   h=project_feasible(h0,n)
   best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   betas=[20,60,180] if n<=350 else [50,150]
   mu=40.0
   for beta in betas:
      if time.time()>deadline-2: break
      x=best_h.copy()
      if beta>betas[0]:
         x=project_feasible(x+0.03*rng.randn(n),n)
      maxfun=300 if n<=350 else 180
      try:
          res=minimize(smooth_obj,x,args=(n,beta,mu),jac=smooth_jac,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':120,'ftol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30})
          x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
          c=exact_c5(x,n)
          if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
      except Exception:
          pass
      # maybe a tiny perturbation and second run if time? Not
   return best_h,best_c
```
Will `smooth_obj` and `smooth_jac` use `n` float target 0.5*n; if n int even. Good.
Bounds list length n could be big but fine.
After continuous, if sum not exact due project, exact_c5 okay.

Potential issue: `minimize` with `jac` expects same return length. Good.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  safe=10.0
  B=budget_s-safe
  final_dead=t0+B
  try:
     base=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
  except Exception:
     base=run_length_start(250,seed).astype(float)
  base_n=len(base)
  base=project_feasible(base,base_n)
  best_h=base; best_n=base_n; best_c=exact_c5(best_h,best_n)
  # choose n list
  ns=[]
  br=int(base_n//2)*2
  if br<2: br=2
  if 150 <= br <= 700: ns.append(br)
  for cand in (250,300,400,500):
     if len(ns)>=5: break
     if cand not in ns and cand>=2: ns.append(cand)
  ns=ns[:4]
  # ensure sorted? maybe [base,250,300...] Not sorted; if base=250 then [250,300,400,500].
  cont_frac=0.10
  final_frac=0.06
  search_frac=1.0-cont_frac-final_frac
  total_w=float(sum(ns))
  t=t0
  deadlines=[]
  for n in ns:
     t += B*search_frac*(n/total_w)
     deadlines.append(t)
  # if no ns? append 250
  seedcur=seed
  for idx,n in enumerate(ns):
     dl=deadlines[idx] - 0.5
     if dl < t0 + 2: continue
     # choose start
     if best_c is None or len(best_h)==0: start=run_length_start(n,seedcur)
     else:
        h1=round_to_binary(interpolate_vec(best_h,n),n)
        h2=run_length_start(n,seedcur+n*13)
        c1=int(corr_int(h1).max()); c2=int(corr_int(h2).max())
        target=int(0.3808*n/2+1e-9)
        if c1 <= max(target,0)+4:
            start=h1
        elif c2 < c1:
            start=h2
        else:
            start=h1
     hb,mb,cb=binary_search(start,n,dl,seedcur+idx*997)
     if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
         best_c=cb; best_h=hb.astype(float); best_n=n
     seedcur += 1000
  # continuous
  t_cont=deadlines[-1] if deadlines else t0
  cont_dead=t_cont + B*cont_frac
  if time.time()<cont_dead-2 and best_h is not None:
      hc,cc=continuous_refine(best_h,best_n,cont_dead,seedcur+4242)
      if cc<best_c-1e-12:
          best_c=cc; best_h=hc.astype(float); best_n=best_n
  # final binary polish
  final_dead=cont_dead + B*final_frac
  if time.time()<final_dead-2 and best_h is not None:
      hb2,mb2,cb2=binary_search(round_to_binary(best_h,best_n),best_n,final_dead,seedcur+7777)
      if cb2<best_c-1e-12:
          best_c=cb2; best_h=hb2.astype(float); best_n=best_n
  # final project if float
  if np.all((best_h==0.0)|(best_h==1.0)):
      final_h=best_h.astype(float)
  else:
      final_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
  final_c=exact_c5(final_h,best_n)
  if final_c<best_c: best_c=final_c
  # ensure time return before t0+budget_s
  if time.time() > t0+budget_s-2 and not np.isfinite(best_c): fallback
  return final_h,best_c,best_n
```

```python
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def exact_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))

def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h
...
```
In project, `np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)` okay. If h has negative zeros. Good.

`round_to_binary`:
```
def round_to_binary(h,n):
    target=n//2
    out=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.int32)
    if target<=0: return out
    idx=np.argsort(-np.asarray(h,dtype=float), kind='mergesort')
    out[idx[:target]]=1
    return out
```
Using `-h` sort avoids `[::-1]`. `kind` maybe okay.

`interpolate_vec`: if n=0? not.
```
def interpolate_vec(v,n):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=float).ravel()
    if len(v)==n: return v.copy()
    if len(v)==0: return np.zeros(n)
    if len(v)==1: return np.full(n,float(v[0]))
    return np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,n), np.linspace(0,1,len(v)), v)
```

`apply_toggle_inplace`: Need L variable not used? for slice end.
```
def apply_toggle_inplace(arr,h,p,b,n,center,L):
    rev=h[::-1]
    if b==0:
        arr[p:p+n] += 1
        arr[p:p+n] -= rev
        arr[center-p:L-p] -= h
    else:
        arr[p:p+n] += rev
        arr[p:p+n] -= 1
        arr[center-p:L-p] += h
    arr[center] -= 1
```
When b=0, second slice subtract h: includes h[p] old0 at center, no correction there; first diag correction arr[center]-=1. But wait arr[center] after both for b0: first diag +1, second diag 0, so subtract once ->0. Good.
When b=1: first diag 0, second diag +1, subtract ->0. Good.

`lag_pairs` returns arrays int64. If no candidates, empty.
```
def lag_pairs(h,s,n):
    empty=np.array([],dtype=np.int32)
    if s>=0:
       if s==0: return empty, empty
       mask=(h[:n-s]==0)&(h[s:]==1)
       q=np.where(mask)[0].astype(np.int32)
       return q, (q+s).astype(np.int32)
    else:
       d=-s
       if d==0: return empty, empty
       mask=(h[:n-d]==1)&(h[d:]==0)
       p=np.where(mask)[0].astype(np.int32)
       return (p+d).astype(np.int32), p
```
`q+s` where q int32, s int64? result int64 maybe; astype.

```
def binary_search(h_start, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = round_to_binary(interpolate_vec(h_start,n), n)
    center = n-1; L=2*n-1
    corr = corr_int(h)
    M = int(corr.max())
    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n
    zeros = np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
    T=3.0; Tend=0.15; rate=(Tend/T)**(1.0/250000.0)
    patience=60000; no_imp=0
    max_allowed = int(0.3808 * n / 2.0 + 1e-9)
    while time.time() < deadline - 0.1:
        if len(zeros)==0 or len(ones)==0: break
        if best_M <= max_allowed and no_imp > 15000: break
        if (no_imp > 3000 and no_imp % 7 == 0) or (rng.random() < 0.12):
            maxidx = np.where(corr == M)[0]
            k = min(2, len(maxidx))
            if k == 1: lags = maxidx
            else: lags = rng.choice(maxidx, k, replace=False)
            bi=-1; bj=-1; bm=M; bcorr=None
            for lag in lags:
                i_list, j_list = lag_pairs(h, int(lag)-center, n)
                cnt = len(i_list)
                if cnt == 0: continue
                take = cnt if cnt <= 12 else 10
                pos = np.arange(cnt) if cnt <=12 else rng.choice(cnt, take, replace=False)
                for pp in pos:
                    ii=int(i_list[pp]); jj=int(j_list[pp])
                    ncorr=swap_new_corr(corr,h,ii,jj,n,center,L)
                    nm=int(ncorr.max())
                    if nm < bm:
                        bm=nm; bi=ii; bj=jj; bcorr=ncorr
            if bi >= 0:
                delta = bm - M
                if delta < 0 or (T > 1e-9 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/T)):
                    h[bi]=1; h[bj]=0; corr=bcorr; M=bm
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n; no_imp=0
                    else:
                        no_imp += 1
                    zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        else:
            ii=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); jj=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            ncorr=swap_new_corr(corr,h,ii,jj,n,center,L)
            nm=int(ncorr.max()); delta=nm-M
            if delta < 0 or (T > 1e-9 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta/T)):
                h[ii]=1; h[jj]=0; corr=ncorr; M=nm
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n; no_imp=0
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
                zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        T = max(Tend, T*rate)
        if no_imp > patience:
            nk = 5 + rng.randint(12)
            for _ in range(nk):
                zidx=int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))]); oidx=int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                h[zidx]=1; h[oidx]=0
            if rng.random() < 0.4:
                bl = 3 + rng.randint(max(1, n//12))
                if bl * 2 < n:
                    a = rng.randint(n-bl)
                    if a < n - 2*bl:
                        b = rng.randint(a+bl, n-bl)
                    elif a - bl > 0:
                        b = rng.randint(0, a-bl)
                    else:
                        b = -1
                    if b >= 0:
                        tmp = h[a:a+bl].copy()
                        h[a:a+bl] = h[b:b+bl]
                        h[b:b+bl] = tmp
            corr = corr_int(h); M=int(corr.max())
            if M < best_M:
                best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=M*2.0/n
            zeros=np.where(h==0)[0]; ones=np.where(h==1)[0]
            no_imp=0; T=min(3.0, T*4.0)
    return best_h.astype(float), best_M, best_c
```

```
def continuous_refine(h0,n,deadline,seed):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h0,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    if n <= 320:
       betas=(30.0,100.0,250.0); maxfun=250
    else:
       betas=(80.0,250.0); maxfun=150
    mu=50.0
    first=True
    for beta in betas:
       if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
       x=best_h.copy()
       if not first:
          x=project_feasible(x + 0.04*rng.randn(n), n)
       first=False
       try:
          res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(n,beta,mu), jac=smooth_jac, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':100,'ftol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20})
          if res.x is not None:
             x=project_feasible(res.x,n)
             c=exact_c5(x,n)
             if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_h, best_c
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def exact_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            s = room.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * room
        else:
            s = h.sum()
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / s) * h
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def round_to_binary(h, n):
    target = n // 2
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    if target <= 0:
        return out
    v = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    idx = np.argsort(-v, kind='mergesort')
    out[idx[:target]] = 1
    return out


def interpolate_vec(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).ravel()
    if len(v) == n:
        return v.copy()
    if len(v) == 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    if len(v) == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(v[0]))
    return np.interp(np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n), np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(v)), v)


def run_length_start(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32)
    bit = rng.randint(2)
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        L = int(rng.geometric(0.45)) + 1
        if L > 6:
            L = 6
        if i + L > n:
            L = n - i
        h[i:i + L] = bit
        i += L
        bit = 1 - bit
    target = n // 2
    cnt = int(h.sum())
    if cnt > target:
        need = cnt - target
        idx = np.where(h == 1)[0]
        h[rng.choice(idx, need, replace=False)] = 0
    elif cnt < target:
        need = target - cnt
        idx = np.where(h == 0)[0]
        h[rng.choice(idx, need, replace=False)] = 1
    return h


def corr_int(h):
    a = h.astype(np.int32, copy=False)
    return np.correlate(a, 1 - a, mode='full').astype(np.int32)


def apply_toggle_inplace(arr, h, p, b, n, center, L):
    rev = h[::-1]
    if b == 0:
        arr[p:p + n] += 1
        arr[p:p + n] -= rev
        arr[center - p:L - p] -= h
    else:
        arr[p:p + n] += rev
        arr[p:p + n] -= 1
        arr[center - p:L - p] += h
    arr[center] -= 1


def swap_new_corr(corr, h, i, j, n, center, L):
    new = corr.copy()
    apply_toggle_inplace(new, h, i, 0, n, center, L)
    apply_toggle_inplace(new, h, j, 1, n, center, L)
    new[center + j - i] += 1
    new[center + i - j] += 1
    return new


def lag_pairs(h, s, n):
    empty = np.array([], dtype=np.int32)
    if s >= 0:
        if s == 0:
            return empty, empty
        mask = (h[:n - s] == 0) & (h[s:] == 1)
        q = np.where(mask)[0].astype(np.int32)
        return q, (q + s).astype(np.int32)
    d = -s
    if d == 0:
        return empty, empty
    mask = (h[:n - d] == 1) & (h[d:] == 0)
    p = np.where(mask)[0].astype(np.int32)
    return (p + d).astype(np.int32), p


def binary_search(h_start, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = round_to_binary(interpolate_vec(h_start, n), n)
    center = n - 1
    L = 2 * n - 1
    corr = corr_int(h)
    M = int(corr.max())
    best_M = M
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = M * 2.0 / n
    zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
    ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
    T = 3.0
    Tend = 0.15
    rate = (Tend / T) ** (1.0 / 250000.0)
    patience = 60000
    no_imp = 0
    max_allowed = int(0.3808 * n / 2.0 + 1e-9)

    while time.time() < deadline - 0.1:
        if len(zeros) == 0 or len(ones) == 0:
            break
        if best_M <= max_allowed and no_imp > 15000:
            break

        if (no_imp > 3000 and no_imp % 7 == 0) or (rng.random() < 0.12):
            maxidx = np.where(corr == M)[0]
            k = min(2, len(maxidx))
            if k == 1:
                lags = maxidx
            else:
                lags = rng.choice(maxidx, k, replace=False)
            bi = -1
            bj = -1
            bm = M
            bcorr = None
            for lag in lags:
                i_list, j_list = lag_pairs(h, int(lag) - center, n)
                cnt = len(i_list)
                if cnt == 0:
                    continue
                if cnt <= 12:
                    pos = np.arange(cnt)
                else:
                    pos = rng.choice(cnt, 10, replace=False)
                for pp in pos:
                    ii = int(i_list[pp])
                    jj = int(j_list[pp])
                    ncorr = swap_new_corr(corr, h, ii, jj, n, center, L)
                    nm = int(ncorr.max())
                    if nm < bm:
                        bm = nm
                        bi = ii
                        bj = jj
                        bcorr = ncorr
            if bi >= 0:
                delta = bm - M
                if delta < 0 or (T > 1e-9 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / T)):
                    h[bi] = 1
                    h[bj] = 0
                    corr = bcorr
                    M = bm
                    if M < best_M:
                        best_M = M
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = M * 2.0 / n
                        no_imp = 0
                    else:
                        no_imp += 1
                    zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
                    ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
            else:
                no_imp += 1
        else:
            ii = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
            jj = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
            ncorr = swap_new_corr(corr, h, ii, jj, n, center, L)
            nm = int(ncorr.max())
            delta = nm - M
            if delta < 0 or (T > 1e-9 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / T)):
                h[ii] = 1
                h[jj] = 0
                corr = ncorr
                M = nm
                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = M * 2.0 / n
                    no_imp = 0
                else:
                    no_imp += 1
                zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
                ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
            else:
                no_imp += 1

        T = max(Tend, T * rate)
        if no_imp > patience:
            nk = 5 + rng.randint(12)
            for _ in range(nk):
                zidx = int(zeros[rng.randint(len(zeros))])
                oidx = int(ones[rng.randint(len(ones))])
                h[zidx] = 1
                h[oidx] = 0
            if rng.random() < 0.4:
                bl = 3 + rng.randint(max(1, n // 12))
                if bl * 2 < n:
                    a = rng.randint(n - bl)
                    if a < n - 2 * bl:
                        b = rng.randint(a + bl, n - bl)
                    elif a - bl > 0:
                        b = rng.randint(0, a - bl)
                    else:
                        b = -1
                    if b >= 0:
                        tmp = h[a:a + bl].copy()
                        h[a:a + bl] = h[b:b + bl]
                        h[b:b + bl] = tmp
            corr = corr_int(h)
            M = int(corr.max())
            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = M * 2.0 / n
            zeros = np.where(h == 0)[0]
            ones = np.where(h == 1)[0]
            no_imp = 0
            T = min(3.0, T * 4.0)

    return best_h.astype(float), best_M, best_c


def smooth_obj(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    q = np.exp(z - zmax)
    val = (zmax + math.log(q.sum())) / beta
    return val + mu * (x.sum() - 0.5 * n) ** 2


def smooth_jac(x, n, beta, mu):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode='full') * dx
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    q = np.exp(z - zmax)
    w = q / q.sum()
    fac = w * dx
    L = 2 * n - 1
    center = n - 1
    first = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    second = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx in range(L):
        acc = fac[idx]
        if acc < 1e-12:
            continue
        s = idx - center
        if s >= 0:
            a = n - s
            first[s:s + a] += acc * (1.0 - x[:a])
            second[:a] += acc * x[s:s + a]
        else:
            d = -s
            a = n - d
            first[:a] += acc * (1.0 - x[d:d + a])
            second[d:d + a] += acc * x[:a]
    pen = 2.0 * mu * (x.sum() - 0.5 * n)
    return first - second + pen


def continuous_refine(h0, n, deadline, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = project_feasible(h0, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = exact_c5(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    if n <= 320:
        betas = (30.0, 100.0, 250.0)
        maxfun = 250
    else:
        betas = (80.0, 250.0)
        maxfun = 150
    mu = 50.0
    first = True
    for beta in betas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2:
            break
        x = best_h.copy()
        if not first:
            x = project_feasible(x + 0.04 * rng.randn(n), n)
        first = False
        try:
            res = minimize(smooth_obj, x, args=(n, beta, mu), jac=smooth_jac,
                           method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                           options={'maxiter': 100, 'ftol': 1e-12,
                                    'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxls': 20})
            if res.x is not None:
                x = project_feasible(res.x, n)
                c = exact_c5(x, n)
                if c < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = c
                    best_h = x.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    global_end = t0 + max(10.0, budget_s - 8.0)

    try:
        base = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        base = None
    if base is None or len(base) == 0:
        base = run_length_start(250, seed).astype(float)
    base = project_feasible(base, len(base))

    best_h = base.copy()
    best_n = len(base)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    ns = [250, 300, 400, 500]
    search_end = t0 + 0.82 * (global_end - t0)
    remaining_ns = ns[:]

    while remaining_ns and time.time() < search_end - 2:
        n = remaining_ns.pop(0)
        rem = max(0.0, search_end - time.time())
        if len(remaining_ns) == 0:
            dl = search_end - 1.0
        else:
            share = n / float(n + sum(remaining_ns))
            dl = time.time() + rem * share - 0.5
            if dl < time.time() + 3:
                dl = time.time() + 3

        h1 = round_to_binary(interpolate_vec(best_h, n), n)
        h2 = run_length_start(n, seed + 1000 * n)
        c1 = int(corr_int(h1).max())
        c2 = int(corr_int(h2).max())
        target = int(0.3808 * n / 2.0 + 1e-9)
        if c1 <= target + 4:
            start = h1
        elif c2 < c1:
            start = h2
        else:
            start = h1

        hb, mb, cb = binary_search(start, n, dl, seed + 9973 * (n % 997))
        if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cb
            best_h = hb.astype(float)
            best_n = n

    final_hard = global_end - 3.0
    rem = max(0.0, final_hard - time.time())
    if rem > 25 and best_h is not None:
        cont_dead = time.time() + 0.5 * rem
        final_dead = time.time() + 0.85 * rem
        hc, cc = continuous_refine(best_h, best_n, cont_dead, seed + 424242)
        if cc < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = cc
            best_h = hc.astype(float)

        if time.time() < final_dead - 5 and best_h is not None:
            hb2, mb2, cb2 = binary_search(round_to_binary(best_h, best_n),
                                          best_n, final_dead, seed + 777777)
            if cb2 < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cb2
                best_h = hb2.astype(float)
                best_n = best_n

    if np.all((best_h == 0.0) | (best_h == 1.0)):
        final_h = best_h.astype(float)
    else:
        final_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = exact_c5(final_h, best_n)
    if final_c < best_c:
        best_c = final_c

    return final_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
